Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the spectroscopy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 587. The resulting operator achieves bound O(90/n^3).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_587(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 587. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^90(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in spectroscopy theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 650 and the invariant I(A,B) = 234 mod 2.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to spectroscopy theory, we decompose the problem into 650 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 234. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
